# Additional per-source analysis and source qualifications {#additional-per-source-causal-analysis}

These supplemental notes record commit-specific engineering observations and evidence limits alongside the main source analyses. Public commit links identify the inspected revisions; no runtime result is independently verified here. A repeated review-scope qualification describes a limit of the analysis, not necessarily a change made by every listed commit.

<a id="commit-100477842650384448f5289e99e96cc15d11464d"></a>
## docs: add CLAUDE.md with project overview, build/setup instructions, and architecture details {#commit-100477842650384448f5289e99e96cc15d11464d}

Analysis: Adds agent-facing Conan/CMake/wheel setup, GTest/pytest entry points, style summaries and boundaries between public headers, reverse-engineered Bedrock ABI, core wrappers, runtime hooks and pybind11. These are documented commands, not evidence that this environment or all stated compiler minima work. No implementation/test change.

<a id="commit-8ee24e2405a7648a4244b96303aefbc9b3a5f84e"></a>
## Merge branch 'v0.10' {#commit-8ee24e2405a7648a4244b96303aefbc9b3a5f84e}



Analysis: Integrates v0.10 BDS 1.21.130 changes into the newer main branch, including packet payload and variant migrations, login validation, nullable-certificate handling, the Linux optimizer flag, flat-map and ABI changes, symbol offsets, resource and shutdown updates, and detached DevTools startup. The commit message identifies conflict paths, and imported code remains attributable through its linked history. This is a bounded merge summary; it does not review every commit inherited from the second parent or establish runtime behavior.

<a id="commit-b187b75e5411e05b26220c2b7145cfe4e129dc4f"></a>
## refactor: update protocol version, and minor code cleanup {#commit-b187b75e5411e05b26220c2b7145cfe4e129dc4f}

Analysis: Protocol859→898; disables changeDimension hook pending correct mapping; console access stops reading removed server_instance.app_ and obtains DedicatedServer* via entt locator, emplaced before original start. Cleanup resets locator<DedicatedServer>, whereas emplacement/get use locator<DedicatedServer*>: distinct specialization mismatch visible in source, not runtime reproduced. Devtools namespace corrected and visitor reformatted.

### Provenance {#commit-b187b75e5411e05b26220c2b7145cfe4e129dc4f-provenance}

Public source: https://github.com/EndstoneMC/endstone/commit/b187b75e5411e05b26220c2b7145cfe4e129dc4f

<a id="commit-d31ef42ce4570804e4e28ffae2e704d2b96d685c"></a>
## refactor: replace no_interactive flag with interactive logic across console and CLI components {#commit-d31ef42ce4570804e4e28ffae2e704d2b96d685c}

Analysis: Replaces negative flag with -i/--interactive/--no-interactive; defaults true Windows, false Linux. Removes Pterodactyl environment heuristic and ENDSTONE_NO_INTERACTIVE click envvar. Bootstrap sets positive ENDSTONE_USE_INTERACTIVE_CONSOLE only for true; C++ initializes replxx only when true. Since environment is copied and false does not erase an inherited positive variable, --no-interactive can retain that variable's effect. No CLI regression tests changed.

### Provenance {#commit-d31ef42ce4570804e4e28ffae2e704d2b96d685c-provenance}
